Results · Definitions

← suite simplipy

sp-acj4-433-cond true

category simplipy-conditional

Summary

Given
\(x \in \mathbb{C},\quad \operatorname{Re}\left(x\right) \ge 0,\quad x \neq 0,\quad -\log x \neq i,\quad -\log x \neq -i,\quad \frac{1}{x} \neq 0,\quad \log\left(\frac{1}{x}\right) \neq i,\quad \log\left(\frac{1}{x}\right) \neq -i\)
Claim
\(\displaystyle \arctan\left(-\log x\right) = \arctan\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)\right)\)
Expected
true equal at every admissible point

the two sides are equal at every admissible point

SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[433]: atan neg log !0 -> atan log inv !0 [weakest assumption found: re-nonneg]

Lean proof: included

Source (conditional.sexp)

(case sp-acj4-433-cond (category simplipy-conditional)
  (note "SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[433]: atan neg log !0  ->  atan log inv !0  [weakest assumption found: re-nonneg]")
  (var x complex)
  (assume (>= (re x) 0))
  (assume (nonzero x))
  (assume (!= (- (log x)) i))
  (assume (!= (- (log x)) (- i)))
  (assume (nonzero (/ x)))
  (assume (!= (log (/ x)) i))
  (assume (!= (log (/ x)) (- i)))
  (ask (= (atan (- (log x))) (atan (log (/ x)))))
  (expect true)
  (lean-proof "have hre : 0 ≤ x.re := (by simpa using h0.2); have hL := AE.cond_log_inv_of_re_nonneg hre; rw [hL] at h5 h6 ⊢; have hD : AE.atan.D (-AE.log x) := (AE.atan.D_iff _).mpr ⟨by rw [← AE.i_def]; exact h2.2, by rw [← AE.i_def]; exact h3.2⟩; exact ⟨⟨⟨h1.2, hD⟩, ⟨h1.2, h4.2⟩, hD⟩, rfl⟩"))

Lean proofs (1; checked by lean-check, each under the statement it proves)

<, ≤, >, ≥ compare real parts (AE.lt a b := a.re < b.re; the side conditions ask for both sides real)

the claim
x ∈ ℂ
h0Re(x) ≥ 0
h1nonzero(x)
h2−log(x) ≠ i
h3−log(x) ≠ −i
h4nonzero(1 / x)
h5log(1 / x) ≠ i
h6log(1 / x) ≠ −i
⊢atan(−log(x)) = atan(log(1 / x))
definedness side conditions (11, and 4 trivially true; t↓: t is defined)
h0Re(x) ∈ ℝ trivially true
h00 ∈ ℝ trivially true
h1nonzero(x)↓ trivially true
h2x ≠ 0
h3x ≠ 0
h4x ≠ 0
h4nonzero(1 / x)↓ trivially true
h5x ≠ 0
h51 / x ≠ 0
h6x ≠ 0
h61 / x ≠ 0
⊢x ≠ 0 ×2
⊢atan(−log(x))↓
⊢1 / x ≠ 0
⊢atan(log(1 / x))↓
Lean statement
theorem case_sp_acj4_433_cond (x : ℂ) (h0 : ((AE.IsReal (AE.re x) ∧ AE.IsReal 0) ∧ (AE.ge (AE.re x) 0))) (h1 : ((AE.nonzero.D x) ∧ (AE.nonzero x))) (h2 : ((x ≠ 0) ∧ (-(AE.log x) ≠ AE.i))) (h3 : ((x ≠ 0) ∧ (-(AE.log x) ≠ -AE.i))) (h4 : ((x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.nonzero.D (AE.inv x)) ∧ (AE.nonzero (AE.inv x)))) (h5 : ((x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.inv x ≠ 0) ∧ (AE.log (AE.inv x) ≠ AE.i))) (h6 : ((x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.inv x ≠ 0) ∧ (AE.log (AE.inv x) ≠ -AE.i))) : (((x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.atan.D (-(AE.log x))) ∧ (x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.inv x ≠ 0) ∧ AE.atan.D (AE.log (AE.inv x))) ∧ (AE.atan (-(AE.log x)) = AE.atan (AE.log (AE.inv x)))) :=
have hre : 0 ≤ x.re := (by simpa using h0.2); have hL := AE.cond_log_inv_of_re_nonneg hre; rw [hL] at h5 h6 ⊢; have hD : AE.atan.D (-AE.log x) := (AE.atan.D_iff _).mpr ⟨by rw [← AE.i_def]; exact h2.2, by rw [← AE.i_def]; exact h3.2⟩; exact ⟨⟨⟨h1.2, hD⟩, ⟨h1.2, h4.2⟩, hD⟩, rfl⟩

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satassume.sympy_api
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satassume')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.refine(Abs(W - 1) + sqrt(W**2), W > 2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

Times: wall-clock seconds per query as the runner measured them (each query in its own forked process; setup and warm-up not timed). Machines: pi5 (indicative): it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise (Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled).

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))
sataskQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))
answernull

# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None

Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): re(x) >= 0

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

sataskQ.zero(Residual)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): re(x) >= 0

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

import sympy

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.refine(Abs(W - 1) + sqrt(W**2), W > 2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

sympy_extras.assumptions.askEq(Residual, 0)→None

# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
read("…/giac_routines.giac");
approx_mode:=0;
complex_variables:=0;
angle_radian:=1;
threads:=1;

// weakened: (>= (re x) 0) (relation of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (- (log x)) i) (!= of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (- (log x)) (- i)) (!= of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (nonzero (/ x)) (!= of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (log (/ x)) i) (!= of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (log (/ x)) (- i)) (!= of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (nonzero x) (!= of a complex variable (Giac stores real intervals only); dropped)
complex_mode:=1;
try { assume(x, complex); aeOut("FACT|0|OK|assume(x, complex)"); } catch (aeErr) { aeOut("FACT|0|ERROR|" + string(aeErr)); };
aeOut("STEP|about(x)|OK|" + string(about(x)));
aeOk := 0;
try { aeLhs := atan((-ln(x))); aeRhs := atan(ln((1/x))); aeDiff := aeLhs - aeRhs; aeOk := 1; } catch (aeErr) { aeFail("L - R", aeErr); };
if (aeOk == 1) { aeStep("L - R", aeDiff); aeSimplified(aeDiff); } else { aeAnswer("unknown", "error"); };
aeOut("END");

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))
answernull

(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] >= 0)) && ((x != 0)) && (((-Log[x]) != I)) && (((-Log[x]) != (-I))) && (((1/x) != 0)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != I)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != (-I)));
  Residual = (ArcTan[-Log[x]]) - (ArcTan[Log[1/x]]);
  Result = Simplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))
satsimp.refinesatsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)→0
answertrue

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] >= 0)) && ((x != 0)) && (((-Log[x]) != I)) && (((-Log[x]) != (-I))) && (((1/x) != 0)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != I)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != (-I)));
  Residual = (ArcTan[-Log[x]]) - (ArcTan[Log[1/x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)
difference(ArcTan[-Log[x]]) - (ArcTan[Log[1/x]])→-ArcTan[Log[x^(-1)]] - ArcTan[Log[x]]

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): re(x) >= 0
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / x)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))
simplifysimplify(Residual)→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

askQ.zero(Residual)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): re(x) >= 0
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / x)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

autoResidual→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))

  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];

)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))
satrefine.refinesatrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)→0

import sympy

# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): re(x) >= 0
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / x)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False)

from sympy import *
import sympy

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): re(x) >= 0
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / x)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))

Result = Residual

Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None

Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))→-atan(log(1/x)) - atan(log(x))
askQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] >= 0)) && ((x != 0)) && (((-Log[x]) != I)) && (((-Log[x]) != (-I))) && (((1/x) != 0)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != I)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != (-I)));
  Residual = (ArcTan[-Log[x]]) - (ArcTan[Log[1/x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)
difference(ArcTan[-Log[x]]) - (ArcTan[Log[1/x]])→-ArcTan[Log[x^(-1)]] - ArcTan[Log[x]]
resultFullSimplify[Residual, Assum]→0

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

satrefine.refinesatrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)→0

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): re(x) >= 0
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / x)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): re(x) >= 0
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / x)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), re(x) >= 0, ~Q.zero(x), Ne(-log(x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(-log(x), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(1 / x), Ne(log(1 / x), I, evaluate=False), Ne(log(1 / x), -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = atan(-log(x)) - atan(log(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] >= 0)) && ((x != 0)) && (((-Log[x]) != I)) && (((-Log[x]) != (-I))) && (((1/x) != 0)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != I)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != (-I)));
  Residual = (ArcTan[-Log[x]]) - (ArcTan[Log[1/x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] >= 0)) && ((x != 0)) && (((-Log[x]) != I)) && (((-Log[x]) != (-I))) && (((1/x) != 0)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != I)) && ((Log[(1/x)] != (-I)));
  Residual = (ArcTan[-Log[x]]) - (ArcTan[Log[1/x]]);
  Result = Refine[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)
